{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nA3: SDF Gradient-Guided Action Refinement via openpi train_loop hooks.\\n\\nExtends A1 (SDF auxiliary task) with gradient-guided action refinement:\\nuses ∂SDF/∂(suffix features) to compute a \\\"collision-safe\\\" action target\\nand trains the model to output it directly.\\n\\nTwo-phase training:\\n  Phase 1 (0 → warmup_phase_steps): Freeze backbone + LoRA. Train SDF decoder +\\n      EE extractor only (~658K params). SDF must be accurate before refinement.\\n  Phase 2 (warmup_phase_steps → end): Unfreeze LoRA (~26M). Enable gradient\\n      refinement. Train with full loss:\\n      Loss = action_loss + λ_sdf × sdf_loss + λ_ee × ee_loss + λ_refine × refine_loss\\n\\nKey difference from A1:\\n  - A1: SDF is an auxiliary loss that improves feature quality\\n  - A3: SDF gradient explicitly shifts action predictions away from collision\\n\\nDependencies:\\n  - openpi train_pytorch.py (TrainHooks, train_loop)\\n  - colliforce/sdf_decoder.py (SDFDecoder, compute_sdf_loss)\\n  - colliforce/ee_extractor.py (EEPositionExtractor)\\n  - colliforce/sdf_gradient_refiner.py (SDFGradientRefiner)\\n  - SDF GT npz (query_points + sdf_values)\\n\\nUsage:\\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 python scripts/train_a3_hook.py --scene spatial_bowl --dry_run\\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 python scripts/train_a3_hook.py --scene spatial_bowl --seed 0\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nimport torch.nn.functional as \\\"src\\\"))\\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), \\\"..\\\"))\\n\\nos.environ[\\\"HF_LEROBOT_HOME\\\"] = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/colliforce_data\\\"\\n\\nimport openpi.models.pi0_config as pi0_config\\nimport openpi.training.config as _config\\nfrom openpi.training.optimizer import CosineDecaySchedule\\nfrom colliforce.sdf_decoder import SDFDecoder, compute_sdf_loss\\nfrom colliforce.ee_extractor import EEPositionExtractor\\nfrom colliforce.sdf_gradient_refiner import SDFGradientRefiner\\nfrom scripts.train_pytorch import TrainHooks, train_loop\\n\\n\\nSCENE_CONFIG = {\\n    \\\"spatial_bowl\\\": {\\n        \\\"description\\\": \\\"SafeLIBERO Spatial: bowl between plate and ramekin\\\",\\n        \\\"data_subdir\\\": \\\"safelibero_spatial\\\",\\n        \\\"sdf_gt\\\": \\\"/root/colliforce-vla/data/sdf_gt_spatial_bowl.npz\\\",\\n    },\\n    \\\"object_pudding\\\": {\\n        \\\"description\\\": \\\"SafeLIBERO Object: pick chocolate pudding into basket\\\",\\n        \\\"data_subdir\\\": \\\"safelibero_object\\\",\\n        \\\"sdf_gt\\\": \\\"/root/colliforce-vla/data/sdf_gt_object_chocolate.npz\\\",\\n    },\\n}\\n\\nCHECKPOINT_BASE = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints\\\"\\nDATA_BASE = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/colliforce_data\\\"\\nWEIGHTS_BASE = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/openpi_weights\\\"\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Manual LoRA — identical to A1 (PEFT not installed)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nclass LoRALinear(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Drop-in nn.Linear replacement with low-rank adapters.\\n    Base weight frozen; only lora_A/lora_B are trainable.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, base: nn.Linear, rank: int = 16, alpha: float = 16.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.base = base\\n        for p in self.base.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n        in_f, out_f = base.in_features, base.out_features\\n        self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(in_f, rank) * 0.01)\\n        self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(rank, out_f))\\n        self.scaling = alpha / rank\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        base_out = self.base(x)\\n        lora_out = (x.float() @ self.lora_A @ self.lora_B) * self.scaling\\n        return base_out + lora_out.to(base_out.dtype)\\n\\n\\ndef _replace_linear(parent: nn.Module, attr_name: str, rank: int, alpha: float):\\n    old = getattr(parent, attr_name)\\n    assert isinstance(old, nn.Linear), f\\\"{attr_name} is {type(old)}, expected nn.Linear\\\"\\n    setattr(parent, attr_name, LoRALinear(old, rank=rank, alpha=alpha))\\n\\n\\nTARGET_MODULES = (\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\",\\n                  \\\"gate_proj\\\", \\\"up_proj\\\", \\\"down_proj\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef inject_lora(model: nn.Module, pali_rank: int = 16, pali_alpha: float = 16.0,\\n                expert_rank: int = 32, expert_alpha: float = 32.0):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Inject LoRA with per-module rank/alpha matching baseline openpi config:\\n    PaliGemma (Gemma 2B) -> rank=16, alpha=16; Action Expert (Gemma 300M) -> rank=32, alpha=32.\\n    Returns (all_lora_params, pali_lora_params, expert_lora_params).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pali_lora_params = []\\n    expert_lora_params = []\\n\\n    # PaliGemma language model layers\\n    pali_layers = model.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.language_model.model.layers\\n    for layer in pali_layers:\\n        for name in TARGET_MODULES:\\n            parent = layer.self_attn if name in (\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\") else layer.mlp\\n            _replace_linear(parent, name, pali_rank, pali_alpha)\\n            lora_mod = getattr(parent, name)\\n            pali_lora_params.extend([lora_mod.lora_A, lora_mod.lora_B])\\n\\n    # Action Expert layers\\n    expert_layers = model.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.model.layers\\n    for layer in expert_layers:\\n        for name in TARGET_MODULES:\\n            parent = layer.self_attn if name in (\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\") else layer.mlp\\n            _replace_linear(parent, name, expert_rank, expert_alpha)\\n            lora_mod = getattr(parent, name)\\n            expert_lora_params.extend([lora_mod.lora_A, lora_mod.lora_B])\\n\\n    all_params = pali_lora_params + expert_lora_params\\n    return all_params, pali_lora_params, expert_lora_params\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# SDF data loading\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef load_sdf_gt(path: str, device: torch.device):\\n    data = np.load(path)\\n    query_points = torch.tensor(data[\\\"query_points\\\"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nA3: SDF Gradient-Guided Action Refinement via openpi train_loop hooks.\\n\\nExtends A1 (SDF auxiliary task) with gradient-guided action refinement:\\nuses ∂SDF/∂(suffix features) to compute a \\\"collision-safe\\\" action target\\nand trains the model to output it directly.\\n\\nTwo-phase training:\\n  Phase 1 (0 → warmup_phase_steps): Freeze backbone + LoRA. Train SDF decoder +\\n      EE extractor only (~658K params). SDF must be accurate before refinement.\\n  Phase 2 (warmup_phase_steps → end): Unfreeze LoRA (~26M). Enable gradient\\n      refinement. Train with full loss:\\n      Loss = action_loss + λ_sdf × sdf_loss + λ_ee × ee_loss + λ_refine × refine_loss\\n\\nKey difference from A1:\\n  - A1: SDF is an auxiliary loss that improves feature quality\\n  - A3: SDF gradient explicitly shifts action predictions away from collision\\n\\nDependencies:\\n  - openpi train_pytorch.py (TrainHooks, train_loop)\\n  - colliforce/sdf_decoder.py (SDFDecoder, compute_sdf_loss)\\n  - colliforce/ee_extractor.py (EEPositionExtractor)\\n  - colliforce/sdf_gradient_refiner.py (SDFGradientRefiner)\\n  - SDF GT npz (query_points + sdf_values)\\n\\nUsage:\\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 python scripts/train_a3_hook.py --scene spatial_bowl --dry_run\\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 python scripts/train_a3_hook.py --scene spatial_bowl --seed 0\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), \\\"..\\\", \\\"openpi\\\"))\\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), \\\"..\\\", \\\"openpi\\\", openpi.models.pi0_config as pi0_config\\nimport openpi.training.config as _config\\nfrom openpi.training.optimizer import CosineDecaySchedule\\nfrom colliforce.sdf_decoder import SDFDecoder, compute_sdf_loss\\nfrom colliforce.ee_extractor import EEPositionExtractor\\nfrom colliforce.sdf_gradient_refiner import SDFGradientRefiner\\nfrom scripts.train_pytorch import TrainHooks, train_loop\\n\\n\\nSCENE_CONFIG = {\\n    \\\"spatial_bowl\\\": {\\n        \\\"description\\\": \\\"SafeLIBERO Spatial: bowl between plate and ramekin\\\",\\n        \\\"data_subdir\\\": \\\"safelibero_spatial\\\",\\n        \\\"sdf_gt\\\": \\\"/root/colliforce-vla/data/sdf_gt_spatial_bowl.npz\\\",\\n    },\\n    \\\"object_pudding\\\": {\\n        \\\"description\\\": \\\"SafeLIBERO },\\n}\\n\\nCHECKPOINT_BASE = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints\\\"\\nDATA_BASE = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/colliforce_data\\\"\\nWEIGHTS_BASE = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/openpi_weights\\\"\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Manual LoRA — identical to A1 (PEFT not installed)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nclass LoRALinear(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Drop-in nn.Linear replacement with low-rank adapters.\\n    Base weight frozen; only lora_A/lora_B are trainable.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, base: nn.Linear, rank: int = 16, alpha: float = 16.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.base = base\\n        for p in self.base.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n        in_f, out_f = base.in_features, base.out_features\\n        self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(in_f, rank) * 0.01)\\n        self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(rank, out_f))\\n        self.scaling = alpha / rank\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        base_out = self.base(x)\\n        lora_out = (x.float() @ self.lora_A @ self.lora_B) * self.scaling\\n        return base_out + lora_out.to(base_out.dtype)\\n\\n\\ndef _replace_linear(parent: nn.Module, attr_name: str, rank: int, alpha: float):\\n    old = getattr(parent, attr_name)\\n    assert isinstance(old, nn.Linear), f\\\"{attr_name} is {type(old)}, expected nn.Linear\\\"\\n    setattr(parent, attr_name, LoRALinear(old, rank=rank, alpha=alpha))\\n\\n\\nTARGET_MODULES = (\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\",\\n                  \\\"gate_proj\\\", \\\"up_proj\\\", \\\"down_proj\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef inject_lora(model: nn.Module, pali_rank: int = 16, pali_alpha: float = 16.0,\\n                expert_rank: int = 32, expert_alpha: float = 32.0):\\n (all_lora_params, pali_lora_params, expert_lora_params).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pali_lora_params = []\\n    expert_lora_params = []\\n\\n    # PaliGemma language model layers\\n    pali_layers = model.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.language_model.model.layers\\n    for layer in pali_layers:\\n        for name in TARGET_MODULES:\\n            parent = layer.self_attn if name in (\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\") else layer.mlp\\n            _replace_linear(parent, name, pali_rank, pali_alpha)\\n            lora_mod = getattr(parent, name)\\n            pali_lora_params.extend([lora_mod.lora_A, lora_mod.lora_B])\\n\\n    # Action Expert layers\\n    expert_layers = model.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.model.layers\\n    for layer in {
  "server": "westd-35807",
  "command": "cat > /root/colliforce-vla/colliforce/lora_utils.py << 'LORA_UTILS_EOF'\n\"\"\"Shared LoRA injection module, extracted from train_a3_hook.py.\n\nProvides manual LoRA (low-rank adaptation) without PEFT dependency.\nLoRALinear wraps nn.Linear with frozen base weights + trainable lora_A/lora_B.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\nTARGET_MODULES = (\"q_proj\", \"k_proj\", \"v_proj\", \"o_proj\",\n                  \"gate_proj\", \"up_proj\", \"down_proj\")\n\n\nclass LoRALinear(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Drop-in nn.Linear replacement with low-rank adapters.\n    Base weight frozen; only lora_A/lora_B are trainable.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, base: nn.Linear, rank: int = 16, alpha: float = 16.0):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.base = base\n        for p in self.base.parameters():\n            p.requires_grad = False\n        in_f, out_f = base.in_features, base.out_features\n        self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(in_f, rank) * 0.01)\n        self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(rank, out_f))\n        self.scaling = alpha / rank\n\n    def forward(self, x):\n        base_out = self.base(x)\n        lora_out = (x.float() @ self.lora_A @ self.lora_B) * self.scaling\n        return base_out + lora_out.to(base_out.dtype)\n\n\ndef inject_lora(module, lora_rank=16, lora_alpha=16.0, target_modules=None):\n    \"\"\"Inject LoRA adapters into matching nn.Linear layers within module.\n\n    Walks all sub-modules. For any nn.Linear whose attribute name matches\n    an entry in target_modules, replaces it with LoRALinear.\n\n    Args:\n        module: nn.Module to inject LoRA into.\n        lora_rank: Rank of the LoRA decomposition.\n        lora_alpha: Scaling factor (effective scale = alpha / rank).\n        target_modules: List of attribute names to replace.\n            Defaults to TARGET_MODULES (q/k/v/o_proj + gate/up/down_proj).\n\n    Returns:\n        List of LoRA parameters (lora_A, lora_B pairs) for the optimizer.\n    \"\"\"\n    if target_modules is None:\n        target_modules = list(TARGET_MODULES)\n\n    lora_params = []\n    for name, child in list(module.named_modules()):\n        if not name:\n            continue\n        parts = name.split(\".\")\n        attr_name = parts[-1]\n        if attr_name in target_modules and isinstance(child, nn.Linear):\n            parent_path = \".\".join(parts[:-1])\n            parent = module.get_submodule(parent_path) if parent_path else module\n            lora_mod = LoRALinear(child, rank=lora_rank, alpha=lora_alpha)\n            setattr(parent, attr_name, lora_mod)\n            lora_params.extend([lora_mod.lora_A, lora_mod.lora_B])\n\n    return lora_params\n\n\ndef inject_lora_pi0(model, pali_rank=16, pali_alpha=16.0,\n                    expert_rank=32, expert_alpha=32.0, target_modules=None):\n    \"\"\"Pi0-specific LoRA injection: separate rank/alpha for backbone vs expert.\n\n    PaliGemma (Gemma 2B) -> pali_rank/pali_alpha\n    Action Expert (Gemma 300M) -> expert_rank/expert_alpha\n\n    Args:\n        model: Pi0 model (unwrapped, not DDP).\n        pali_rank: LoRA rank for PaliGemma backbone.\n        pali_alpha: LoRA alpha for PaliGemma backbone.\n        expert_rank: LoRA rank for action expert.\n        expert_alpha: LoRA alpha for action expert.\n        target_modules: Override target module names.\n\n    Returns:\n        (all_lora_params, pali_lora_params, expert_lora_params)\n    \"\"\"\n    pali_model = model.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.language_model.model\n    expert_model = model.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.model\n\n    pali_lora_params = inject_lora(pali_model, lora_rank=pali_rank,\n                                   lora_alpha=pali_alpha,\n                                   target_modules=target_modules)\n    expert_lora_params = inject_lora(expert_model, lora_rank=expert_rank,\n                                     lora_alpha=expert_alpha,\n                                     target_modules=target_modules)\n\n    all_params = pali_lora_params + expert_lora_params\n    logging.info(\"LoRA injected: PaliGemma rank=%d (%d params), Expert rank=%d (%d params)\",\n                 pali_rank, sum(p.numel() for p in pali_lora_params),\n                 expert_rank, sum(p.numel() for p in expert_lora_params))\n    return all_params, pali_lora_params, expert_lora_params\nLORA_UTILS_EOF\necho \"lora_utils.py written OK\"",

}

\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints\"\nDATA_BASE = \"/root/autodl-tmp/colliforce_data\"\nWEIGHTS_BASE = \"/root/autodl-tmp/openpi_weights\"\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    p = p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=3)\n    p.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", type=int, default=32)\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_train_steps\", type=int, default=30000)\n    p.add_argument(\"--lr\", type=float, default=2.5e-5)\n    p.add_argument(\"--save_interval\", type=int, default=2000)\n    p.add_argument(\"--log_interval\", type=int, default=100)\n    p.add_argument(\"--exp_name\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--dry_run\", action=\"store_true\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--resume\", action=\"store_true\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--lambda_sdf\", type=float, default=0.1)\n    p.add_argument(\"--lambda_ee\", type=float, default=0.01)\n    p.add_argument(\"--n_query_points\", type=int, default=64)\n    p.add_argument(\"--sdf_clamp\", type=float, default=0.05)\n    p.add_argument(\"--eikonal_weight\", type=float, default=0.1)\n    p.add_argument(\"--warmup_phase_steps\", type=int, default=5000)\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\nclass SuffixOutHook:\n    \"\"\"Captures input to action_out_proj (= suffix_out after slicing).\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.features = None\n\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\n        self.features = input[0]  # [B, action_horizon, 1024]\n\n\ndef load_sdf_gt(path, device):\n    data = np.load(path)\n    query_points = torch.tensor(data[\"query_points\"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device)\n    sdf_values = torch.tensor(data[\"sdf_values\"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device)\n    return query_points, sdf_values\n\n\ndef sample_sdf_batch(query_points, sdf_values, batch_size, n_query):\n    indices = torch.randint(0, len(query_points), (n_query,), device=query_points.device)\n    pts = query_points[indices].unsqueeze(0).expand(batch_size, -1, -1)\n    sdf = sdf_values[indices].unsqueeze(0).expand(batch_size, -1)\n    return pts, sdf\n\n\nclass SdfAuxHooks(TrainHooks):\n    def __init__(self, args):\n        self.args = args\n        self.phase = 1\n        self.warmup_phase_steps = args.warmup_phase_steps\n        self.lambda_sdf = args.lambda_sdf\n        self.lambda_ee = args.lambda_ee\n        self.n_query_points = args.n_query_points\n\n        self.sdf_decoder = SDFDecoder(feature_dim=1024, hidden_dim=256)\n        self.ee_extractor = EEPositionExtractor(feature_dim=1024, hidden_dim=256)\n\n        self.suffix_hook = SuffixOutHook()\n        self.hook_handle = None\n        self.sdf_query = None\n        self.sdf_values = None\n\n    def on_model_created(self, model, device):\n        _m = model.module if hasattr(model, \"module\") else model\n        self.hook_handle = _m.action_out_proj.register_forward_hook(self.suffix_hook)\n\n        self.sdf_decoder = self.sdf_decoder.to(device)\n        self.ee_extractor = self.ee_extractor.to(device)\n\n        scene_cfg = SCENE_CONFIG[self.args.scene]\n        self.sdf_query, self.sdf_values = load_sdf_gt(scene_cfg[\"sdf_gt\"], device)\n        logging.info(\"SDF GT loaded: %d points\", len(self.sdf_query))\n\n        # Freeze ALL backbone params\n        for p in _m.parameters():\n            p.requires_grad = False\n\n        # Inject LoRA (initially frozen for Phase 1)\n        self.lora_params, self._pali_lora_params, self._expert_lora_params = inject_lora_pi0(\n            _m, pali_rank=16, pali_alpha=16.0, expert_rank=32, expert_alpha=32.0)\n        for p in self.lora_params:\n            p.requires_grad = False\n\n        # Unfreeze aux modules\n        for p in self.sdf_decoder.parameters():\n            p.requires_grad = True\n        for p in self.ee_extractor.parameters():\n            p.requires_grad = True\n\n        n_backbone = sum(p.numel() for p in _m.parameters())\n        n_lora = sum(p.numel() for p in self.lora_params)\n        n_sdf = sum(p.numel() for p in self.sdf_decoder.parameters())\n        n_ee = sum(p.numel() for p in self.ee_extractor.parameters())\n        logging.info(\"=== A1 SDF Aux Hooks ===\")\n        logging.info(\"  Backbone params (incl. LoRA weights): %.2fM\", n_backbone / 1e6)\n        logging.info(\"  LoRA PaliGemma (rank=16): %.2fM\",\n                     sum(p.numel() for p in self._pali_lora_params) / 1e6)\n        logging.info(\"  LoRA Action Expert (rank=32): %.2fM\",\n                     sum(p.numel() for p in self._expert_lora_params) / 1e6)\n        logging.info(\"  LoRA total (frozen for Phase 1): %.2fM\", n_lora / 1e6)\n        logging.info(\"  Phase 1: SDF decoder (%d) + EE extractor (%d) trainable\",\n                     n_sdf, n_ee)\n        logging.info(\"  Phase 2: + LoRA (%.2fM) will be unfrozen\", n_lora / 1e6)\n\n    def extra_params(self):\n        return list(self.sdf_decoder.parameters()) + list(self.ee_extractor.parameters())\n\n    def on_step_start(self, global_step, model):\n        if global_step == self.warmup_phase_steps and self.phase == 1:\n            self.phase = 2\n            self._phase2_start = global_step\n            self._lora_warmup_steps = 100\n            self._lora_warmup_scale = 0.0\n            self._lora_grad_hooks = []\n            n_unfrozen = 0\n            for p in self.lora_params:\n                p.requires_grad = True\n                n_unfrozen += p.numel()\n                h = p.register_hook(lambda grad, s=self: grad * s._lora_warmup_scale)\n                self._lora_grad_hooks.append(h)\n            n_total_trainable = n_unfrozen\n            for p in self.sdf_decoder.parameters():\n                n_total_trainable += p.numel()\n            for p in self.ee_extractor.parameters():\n                n_total_trainable += p.numel()\n            logging.info(\n                \"Phase 2: unfroze %d LoRA params at step %d (total trainable: %d, LoRA grad warmup: %d steps)\",\n                n_unfrozen, global_step, n_total_trainable, self._lora_warmup_steps,\n            )\n\n        if self.phase == 2 and hasattr(self, '_phase2_start'):\n            steps_in = global_step - self._phase2_start\n            if steps_in < self._lora_warmup_steps:\n                self._lora_warmup_scale = (steps_in + 1) / self._lora_warmup_steps\n            elif hasattr(self, '_lora_grad_hooks') and self._lora_grad_hooks:\n                for h in self._lora_grad_hooks:\n                    h.remove()\n                self._lora_grad_hooks = []\n                self._lora_warmup_scale = 1.0\n                logging.info(\"LoRA gradient warmup complete at step %d\", global_step)\n\n        # Param verification for first 100 steps\n        if global_step < 100 and global_step % 10 == 0:\n            _m = model.module if hasattr(model, \"module\") else model\n            trainable = {n: p.numel() for n, p in _m.named_parameters() if p.requires_grad}\n            aux_trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in self.sdf_decoder.parameters() if p.requires_grad) + \\\n                           sum(p.numel() for p in self.ee_extractor.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\n            print(f\"[Step {global_step}] Trainable params: {sum(trainable.values()) + aux_trainable:,}\")\n            print(f\"[Step {global_step}] LoRA params: {sum(v for n, v in trainable.items() if 'lora' in n):,}\")\n\n    def on_loss_computed(self, action_loss, model, observation, global_step):\n        features = self.suffix_hook.features\n        if features is None:\n            return action_loss, {}\n\n        pooled = features.mean(dim=1)  # [B, 1024]\n        if self.phase == 1:\n            pooled = pooled.detach()\n\n        B = pooled.shape[0]\n        query_pts, gt_sdf = sample_sdf_batch(\n            self.sdf_query, self.sdf_values, B, self.n_query_points\n        )\n        pred_sdf, grad_sdf = self.sdf_decoder(pooled, query_pts, return_gradients=True)\n        sdf_losses = compute_sdf_loss(\n            pred_sdf, gt_sdf, grad_sdf,\n            clamp_delta=self.args.sdf_clamp,\n            eikonal_weight=self.args.eikonal_weight,\n        )\n\n        ee_gt = observation.state[:, :3].float()\n        ee_pred = self.ee_extractor(pooled)\n        ee_loss = F.mse_loss(ee_pred, ee_gt)\n\n        total = action_loss + self.lambda_sdf * sdf_losses[\"sdf_total\"] + self.lambda_ee * ee_loss\n\n        aux_info = {\n            \"action_loss\": action_loss.item(),\n            \"sdf_l1\": sdf_losses[\"sdf_l1\"].item(),\n            \"sdf_total\": sdf_losses[\"sdf_total\"].item(),\n            \"ee_loss\": ee_loss.item(),\n            \"phase\": float(self.phase),\n        }\n        if \"eikonal\" in sdf_losses:\n            aux_info[\"eikonal\"] = sdf_losses[\"eikonal\"].item()\n\n        return total, aux_info\n\n    def on_save_checkpoint(self, step_dir, global_step):\n        torch.save(self.sdf_decoder.state_dict(), os.path.join(step_dir, \"sdf_decoder.pt\"))\n        torch.save(self.ee_extractor.state_dict(), os.path.join(step_dir, \"ee_extractor.pt\"))\n        torch.save({\"phase\": self.phase, \"global_step\": global_step},\n                    os.path.join(step_dir, \"aux_metadata.pt\"))\n        logging.info(\"Saved aux modules at step %d\", global_step)\n\n    def on_cleanup(self):\n        if self.hook_handle is not None:\n            self.hook_handle.remove()\n        for h in getattr(self, '_lora_grad_hooks', []):\n            h.remove()\n\n\ndef build_config(args):\n    scene_cfg = SCENE_CONFIG[args.scene]\n    num_steps = 2 if args.dry_run else args.num_train_steps\n    exp_name = args.exp_name or f\"a1_sdf_{args.scene}_seed{args.seed}\"\n\n    model_config = pi0_config.Pi0Config(\n        paligemma_variant=\"gemma_2b\",\n        action_expert_variant=\"gemma_300m\",\n    )\n\n    config = _config.TrainConfig(\n        name=f\"a1_sdf_{args.scene}\",\n        project_name=\"colliforce-vla\",\n        exp_name=exp_name,\n        model=model_config,\n        data=_config.LeRobotLiberoDataConfig(\n            repo_id=scene_cfg[\"data_subdir\"],\n            base_config=_config.DataConfig(prompt_from_task=True),\n            extra_delta_transform=False,\n            assets=AssetsConfig(assets_dir=scene_cfg[\"assets_dir\"]),\n        ),\n        pytorch_weight_path=os.path.join(WEIGHTS_BASE, \"pi0_base\"),\n        ema_decay=None,\n        lr_schedule=CosineDecaySchedule(\n            warmup_steps=min(1000, num_steps // 3),\n            peak_lr=args.lr,\n            decay_steps=num_steps,\n            decay_lr=args.lr / 10,\n        ),\n        num_train_steps=num_steps,\n        batch_size=args.batch_size,\n        save_interval=args.save_interval if not args.dry_run else num_steps,\n        log_interval=args.log_interval if not args.dry_run else 1,\n        checkpoint_base_dir=CHECKPOINT_BASE,\n        seed=args.seed,\n        wandb_enabled=not args.dry_run,\n        overwrite=args.dry_run,\n        resume=args.resume,\n    )\n    return config\n\n\ndef main():\n    args = parse_args()\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\")\n\n    if not int(os.environ.get(\"WORLD_SIZE\", \"1\")) > 1:\n        os.environ[\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\"] = str(args.gpu)\n\n    logging.info(\"A1 SDF Aux (hooks) | Scene: %s | Seed: %d | Dry run: %s\",\n                 args.scene, args.seed, args.dry_run)\n    logging.info(\"Phase 1 warmup: %d steps, lambda_sdf=%.3f, lambda_ee=%.3f\",\n                 args.warmup_phase_steps, args.lambda_sdf, args.lambda_ee)\n\n    config = build_config(args)\n    hooks = SdfAuxHooks(args)\n    train_loop(config, hooks=hooks)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nA1_EOF\necho \"train_a1_sdf_aux.py written OK\"",

## 产出汇报

### 1. lora_utils.py 完整内容

`/root/colliforce-vla/colliforce/lora_utils.py` — 包含：
- `LoRALinear(nn.Module)` — 冻结 base weight，只训练 lora_A/lora_B
- `inject_lora(module, lora_rank, lora_alpha, target_modules)` — 通用函数，遍历任意 nn.Module 替换匹配的 nn.Linear
- `inject_lora_pi0(model, pali_rank, pali_alpha, expert_rank, expert_alpha)` — Pi0 专用便捷函数，分别对 PaliGemma 和 Action Expert 注入不同 rank

### 2. train_baseline.py 修改 diff 摘要

- 新增 `sys.path.insert` 父目录（for colliforce imports）
- 新增 import: `TrainHooks`, `train_loop`, `inject_lora_pi0`
- 新增 `BaselineLoRAHooks(TrainHooks)` 类：
  - `on_model_created`: 注入 LoRA，冻结所有非 LoRA 参数
  - `on_step_start`: 前 100 步每 10 步打印可训练参数统计
- `build_config`: `gemma_2b_lora` → `gemma_2b`, `gemma_300m_lora` → `gemma_300m`, 移除 `freeze_filter`
- `main()`: 创建 `BaselineLoRAHooks()` 并传给 `train_loop(config, hooks=hooks)`

### 3. train_a1_sdf_aux.py 修改 diff 摘要

- 新增 import: `from colliforce.lora_utils import inject_lora_pi0`
- `on_model_created`: 冻结所有参数后，调用 `inject_lora_pi0` 注入 LoRA（Phase 1 保持冻结），然后解冻 SDF decoder + EE extractor
- `on_step_start`: Phase 2 改为直接遍历 `self.lora_params` 解冻（不再用 name-based 搜索）；新增前 100 步参数验证打印
- `build_config`: `gemma_2b_lora` → `gemma_2b`, `gemma_300m_lora` → `gemma_300m`, 移除 `freeze_filter`

### 4. A3 实际使用的 LoRA 配置

| 参数 | PaliGemma (Gemma 2B) | Action Expert (Gemma 300M) |
|------|---------------------|---------------------------|
| rank | 16 | 32 |
| alpha | 16.0 | 32.0 |
| target_modules | q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj, gate_proj, up_proj, down_proj | 同左 |

### 5. 验证结果

| 测试 | 结果 |
|------|------|
| `from colliforce.lora_utils import inject_lora` | OK |
| `python scripts/train_baseline.py --help` | OK |
| `python scripts/train_a1_sdf_aux.py --help` | OK |
| inject_lora 功能测试（FakeModel） | PASSED — 2 层 × 4 模块 × 2 参数 = 16 LoRA params |

## 产出汇报

### 1. lora_utils.py 完整内容

`/root/colliforce-vla/colliforce/lora_utils.py` — 包含：
- `LoRALinear(nn.Module)` — 冻结 base weight，只训练 lora_A/lora_B
- `inject_lora(module, lora_rank, lora_alpha, target_modules)` — 通用函数，遍历任意 nn.Module 替换匹配的 nn.Linear
- `inject_lora_pi0(model, pali_rank, pali_alpha, expert_rank, expert_alpha)` — Pi0 专用便捷函数，分别对 PaliGemma 和 Action Expert 注入不同 rank

### 2. train_baseline.py 修改 diff 摘要

- 新增 `sys.path.insert` 父目录（for colliforce imports）
- 新增 import: `TrainHooks`, `train_loop`, `inject_lora_pi0`
- 新增 `BaselineLoRAHooks(TrainHooks)` 类：
  - `on_model_created`: 注入 LoRA，冻结所有非 LoRA 参数
  - `on_step_start`: 前 100 步每 10 步打印可训练参数统计
- `build_config`: `gemma_2b_lora` → `gemma_2b`, `gemma_300m_lora` → `gemma_300m`, 移除 `freeze_filter`
- `main()`: 创建 `BaselineLoRAHooks()` 并传给 `train_loop(config, hooks=hooks)`

### 3. train_a1_sdf_aux.py 修改 diff 摘要

- 新增 import: `from colliforce.lora_utils import inject_lora_pi0`
- `on_model_created`: 冻结所有参数后，调用 `inject_lora_pi0` 注入 LoRA（Phase 1 保持冻结），然后解冻 SDF decoder + EE extractor
- `on_step_start`: Phase 2 改为直接遍历 `self.lora_params` 解冻（不再用 name-based 搜索）；新增前 100 步参数验证打印
- `build_config`: `gemma_2b_lora` → `gemma_2b`, `gemma_300m_lora` → `gemma_300m`, 移除 `freeze_filter`

### 4. A3 实际使用的 LoRA 配置

| 参数 | PaliGemma (Gemma 2B) | Action Expert (Gemma 300M) |
|------|---------------------|---------------------------|
| rank | 16 | 32 |
| alpha | 16.0 | 32.0 |
| target_modules | q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj, gate_proj, up_proj, down_proj | 同左 |
